<script type="text/javascript">

    function ajaxSubmit(token) {
        
        if ($('#Message').val().length > 0) {

            var settings = {
                data: $("#contactForm").serialize(),
                url: $("#contactForm").attr('action'),
                type: $("#contactForm").attr('method')
            };

            $.ajax(settings).done(function (result) {

                if (result.Success) {
                    $.toaster({  message: 'Your message was sent successfully. A member of the team will contact you by email shortly.', title: 'Contact email', priority: 'success'  });

                } else {
                    $.toaster({ message: 'Your message was not sent successfully', title: 'Contact email', priority: 'danger'});
         
                }

            });
        }

        $('#contactModal').modal('hide');

        return false;
    }
    function validateEmail(email) {
        var pattern = /^([a-z\d!#$%&'*+\-\/=?^_`{|}~\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]+(\.[a-z\d!#$%&'*+\-\/=?^_`{|}~\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]+)*|"((([ \t]*\r\n)?[ \t]+)?([\x01-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f\x21\x23-\x5b\x5d-\x7e\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]|\\[\x01-\x09\x0b\x0c\x0d-\x7f\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]))*(([ \t]*\r\n)?[ \t]+)?")@(([a-z\d\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]|[a-z\d\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF][a-z\d\-._~\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]*[a-z\d\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])\.)+([a-z\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]|[a-z\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF][a-z\d\-._~\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]*[a-z\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])\.?$/i;
        return pattern.test(email);
    }

    function validate(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        $("#result").text("");
        var email = $("#UserEmail").val();
        if (!validateEmail(email)) {
            $("#result").text("Bad email format");
            $("#result").css("color", "red");
            return false;
        }
        grecaptcha.execute();
        return true;
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {

        var element = document.getElementById('contactSubmit');
        element.onclick = validate;

    });
</script>

<td class="green" ng-class="{green : isAmountGreaterThenZero(profitLossBackWin), red : isAmountLessThenZero(profitLossBackWin)}"><span class="ng-binding">£0.00</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="profit-exchange">If Exchange Lay Bet Wins</td>
<td><span class="red ng-binding">£0.00</span></td>
<td><span class="green ng-binding">£0.00</span></td>

</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<div class="col-md-5 col-md-push-8 col-sm-5 col-sm-push-7 col-xs-12 abt-img text-center">
<img alt="img" class="img-responsive img-1 visible-xs" src="/Content/img/common/matched-betting-app-both.png"/>

<div class="col-md-7 col-md-pull-4 col-sm-7 col-sm-pull-5 col-xs-12 abt-text">
<h1>What is Matched Betting?</h1>
<p>Firstly unlike normal betting, matched betting isn’t gambling since there’s no risk involved. Matched betting is a legal loophole and it works on the principles of basic maths. Online bookmakers offer free bets and bonuses to attract new customers, or encourage existing customers to have another bet with them, you know the sort of thing “Bet £50 with us and we’ll give you a £50 free bet” </p> <p>Well matched Betting allows you to take advantage of these free bonus offers and in turn cash these free bet offers out for guaranteed profits. This is done by placing risk free matched bets; bets that cover all outcomes in a betting event and remove all the normal risk.</p> <p>In a nutshell matched betting allows you to profit from any bookmaker’s free bet or bonus offer, withdraw your profits and then move onto the next bookmaker that’s offering you a bonus to repeat the process when you’re done. You can make upto £1500 every month from matched betting and the best part is its all risk free. Don’t take our word for it, see what ‘ The Guardian ’ newspaper had to say about us below …</p>
</div><!-- abt-text -->
</div><!-- row -->
</div><!-- container -->
</div><!-- abt-sec -->
